Results · Definitions

← suite simplipy

sp-acj4-5-cond true

category simplipy-conditional

Summary

Given
\(x \in \mathbb{R},\quad \left|x\right| < 1,\quad \left|x\right| \neq 1,\quad x \neq 1,\quad x \neq -1\)
Claim
\(\displaystyle \operatorname{artanh}\left(\left|x\right|\right) = \left|\operatorname{artanh}\left(x\right)\right|\)
Expected
true equal at every admissible point

the two sides are equal at every admissible point

SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[5] + rules.json[3]: atanh abs _0 -> abs atanh _0 [weakest assumption found: abs-lt-1]

Lean proof: included

Source (conditional.sexp)

(case sp-acj4-5-cond (category simplipy-conditional)
  (note "SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[5] + rules.json[3]: atanh abs _0  ->  abs atanh _0  [weakest assumption found: abs-lt-1]")
  (var x real)
  (assume (< (abs x) 1))
  (assume (!= (abs x) 1))
  (assume (!= x 1))
  (assume (!= x -1))
  (ask (= (atanh (abs x)) (abs (atanh x))))
  (expect true)
  (lean-proof "obtain ⟨r, rfl⟩ := AE.IsReal.exists hd0; have hr : |r| < 1 := (by simpa [AE.abs_ofReal] using h0.2); have hr' := abs_lt.mp hr; refine ⟨by simp [AE.abs_ofReal, h2, h3]; exact ⟨by intro h; linarith, by intro h; have := congrArg Complex.re h; simp at this; linarith [abs_nonneg r]⟩, ?_⟩; rw [AE.abs_ofReal, AE.atanh_ofReal (by linarith [abs_nonneg r]) hr, AE.atanh_ofReal hr'.1 hr'.2, AE.abs_ofReal, AE.cond_artanh_abs]"))

Lean proofs (1; checked by lean-check, each under the statement it proves)

<, ≤, >, ≥ compare real parts (AE.lt a b := a.re < b.re; the side conditions ask for both sides real)

the claim
x ∈ ℂ
hd0x ∈ ℝ
h0|x| < 1
h1|x| ≠ 1
h2x ≠ 1
h3x ≠ −1
⊢atanh(|x|) = |atanh(x)|
definedness side conditions (3, and 5 trivially true; t↓: t is defined)
h0|x|↓ trivially true
h0|x| ∈ ℝ
h01 ∈ ℝ trivially true
h1|x|↓ trivially true
⊢|x|↓ trivially true
⊢atanh(|x|)↓
⊢atanh(x)↓
⊢|atanh(x)|↓ trivially true
Lean statement
theorem case_sp_acj4_5_cond (x : ℂ) (hd0 : AE.IsReal x) (h0 : ((AE.abs.D x ∧ AE.IsReal (AE.abs x) ∧ AE.IsReal 1) ∧ (AE.lt (AE.abs x) 1))) (h1 : ((AE.abs.D x) ∧ (AE.abs x ≠ 1))) (h2 : (x ≠ 1)) (h3 : (x ≠ -1)) : (((AE.abs.D x ∧ AE.atanh.D (AE.abs x)) ∧ AE.atanh.D x ∧ AE.abs.D (AE.atanh x)) ∧ (AE.atanh (AE.abs x) = AE.abs (AE.atanh x))) :=
obtain ⟨r, rfl⟩ := AE.IsReal.exists hd0; have hr : |r| < 1 := (by simpa [AE.abs_ofReal] using h0.2); have hr' := abs_lt.mp hr; refine ⟨by simp [AE.abs_ofReal, h2, h3]; exact ⟨by intro h; linarith, by intro h; have := congrArg Complex.re h; simp at this; linarith [abs_nonneg r]⟩, ?_⟩; rw [AE.abs_ofReal, AE.atanh_ofReal (by linarith [abs_nonneg r]) hr, AE.atanh_ofReal hr'.1 hr'.2, AE.abs_ofReal, AE.cond_artanh_abs]

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satassume.sympy_api
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satassume')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Answer = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum, strict=True)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

satsimp.refinesatsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
answernull

import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.trigsimp(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = satsimp.trigsimp(Residual, Assum, strict=True)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
satsimp.trigsimpsatsimp.trigsimp(Residual, Assum, strict=True)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

askQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.askEq(Residual, 0)→None
answernull

Times: wall-clock seconds per query as the runner measured them (each query in its own forked process; setup and warm-up not timed). Machines: pi5 (indicative): it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise (Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled).

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.trigsimp(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Abs[x] < 1)) && ((Abs[x] != 1)) && ((x != 1)) && ((x != (-1)));
  Residual = (ArcTanh[Abs[x]]) - (Abs[ArcTanh[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)
difference(ArcTanh[Abs[x]]) - (Abs[ArcTanh[x]])→-Abs[ArcTanh[x]] + ArcTanh[Abs[x]]

import importlib.metadata

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))

# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number

W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

x = Symbol('x')

W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))

import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)

approx_mode:=0;
complex_variables:=0;
angle_radian:=1;
threads:=1;

// weakened: (< (abs x) 1) (relation of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (abs x) 1) (!= of an expression; dropped)
complex_mode:=0;
try { assume(x < 1 or x > 1); aeOut("FACT|0|OK|assume(x < 1 or x > 1)"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|0|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
try { additionally(x < (-1) or x > (-1)); aeOut("FACT|1|OK|additionally(x < (-1) or x > (-1))"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|1|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
aeOut("STEP|about(x)|OK|" + string(about(x)));
aeOk := 0;
try { aeLhs := atanh(abs(x)); aeRhs := abs(atanh(x)); aeDiff := aeLhs - aeRhs; aeOk := 1; } catch (aeErr) { aeFail("L - R", aeErr); };
if (aeOk == 1) { aeStep("L - R", aeDiff); aeSimplified(aeDiff); } else { aeAnswer("unknown", "error"); };
aeOut("END");

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
read("…/giac_routines.giac");
approx_mode:=0;
complex_variables:=0;
angle_radian:=1;
threads:=1;

// weakened: (< (abs x) 1) (relation of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (abs x) 1) (!= of an expression; dropped)
complex_mode:=0;
try { assume(x < 1 or x > 1); aeOut("FACT|0|OK|assume(x < 1 or x > 1)"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|0|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
try { additionally(x < (-1) or x > (-1)); aeOut("FACT|1|OK|additionally(x < (-1) or x > (-1))"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|1|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
aeOut("STEP|about(x)|OK|" + string(about(x)));
aeOk := 0;
try { aeLhs := atanh(abs(x)); aeRhs := abs(atanh(x)); aeDiff := aeLhs - aeRhs; aeOk := 1; } catch (aeErr) { aeFail("L - R", aeErr); };
if (aeOk == 1) { aeStep("L - R", aeDiff); aeEvaluated(aeDiff); } else { aeAnswer("unknown", "error"); };
aeOut("END");

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = simplify(satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum, strict=True))

print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

try { aeLhs := atanh(abs(x)); aeRhs := abs(atanh(x)); aeDiff := aeLhs - aeRhs; aeOk := 1; } catch (aeErr) { aeFail("L - R", aeErr); };
if (aeOk == 1) { aeStep("L - R", aeDiff); aeEvaluated(aeDiff); } else { aeAnswer("unknown", "error"); };
aeOut("END");

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satassume.sympy_api
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satassume')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W), strict=True)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Answer = satassume.sympy_api.ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum, strict=True)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
satassume.sympy_api.askQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Abs[x] < 1)) && ((Abs[x] != 1)) && ((x != 1)) && ((x != (-1)));
  Residual = (ArcTanh[Abs[x]]) - (Abs[ArcTanh[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

satsimp.refinesatsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Abs(x) < 1
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False)
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
askQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Abs(x) < 1
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False)
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

complex_variables:=0;
angle_radian:=1;
threads:=1;

// weakened: (< (abs x) 1) (relation of an expression; dropped)
// weakened: (!= (abs x) 1) (!= of an expression; dropped)
complex_mode:=0;
try { assume(x < 1 or x > 1); aeOut("FACT|0|OK|assume(x < 1 or x > 1)"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|0|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
try { additionally(x < (-1) or x > (-1)); aeOut("FACT|1|OK|additionally(x < (-1) or x > (-1))"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|1|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
aeOut("STEP|about(x)|OK|" + string(about(x)));
aeOk := 0;
try { aeLhs := atanh(abs(x)); aeRhs := abs(atanh(x)); aeDiff := aeLhs - aeRhs; aeOk := 1; } catch (aeErr) { aeFail("L - R", aeErr); };
if (aeOk == 1) { aeStep("L - R", aeDiff); aeSimplified(aeDiff); } else { aeAnswer("unknown", "error"); };
aeOut("END");

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))

from sympy import *
import sympy

import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings

# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None

W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))

Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))

Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))

# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

if (aeOk == 1) { aeStep("L - R", aeDiff); aeEvaluated(aeDiff); } else { aeAnswer("unknown", "error"); };
aeOut("END");

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))

  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Abs[x] < 1)) && ((Abs[x] != 1)) && ((x != 1)) && ((x != (-1)));
  Residual = (ArcTanh[Abs[x]]) - (Abs[ArcTanh[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
satsimp.refinesatsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

askQ.zero(Residual)→None

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Abs(x) < 1
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False)
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))

import sympy

import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))

# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))

Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
sympy_extras.assumptions.askEq(Residual, 0)→None

import sympy

import satsimp
import importlib.metadata

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Abs(x) < 1
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False)

import sympy

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

satrefine.refinesatrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Abs[x] < 1)) && ((Abs[x] != 1)) && ((x != 1)) && ((x != (-1)));
  Residual = (ArcTanh[Abs[x]]) - (Abs[ArcTanh[x]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)
difference(ArcTanh[Abs[x]]) - (Abs[ArcTanh[x]])→-Abs[ArcTanh[x]] + ArcTanh[Abs[x]]

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
satrefine.refinesatrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)→-Abs(atanh(x)) + atanh(Abs(x))
answernull

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Abs(x) < 1, Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False), Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False))
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Abs(x) < 1
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(Abs(x), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(x, -1, evaluate=False)
Residual = atanh(Abs(x)) - Abs(atanh(x))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
